The compile-testing:0.7 test dependency (used by auto-common module) declares a system-scoped dependency on com.sun:tools:jar\n2. This dependency references ${toolsjar} property which points to ${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar\n3. The profile that sets this property activates when the file exists: <exists>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</exists>\n4. On Java 11, this file doesn't exist, so the property gets set but points to a non-existent path\n5.
